# Changelog

What changed, build by build, newest first. Each entry names its commit (`git log`); the full account of a change is in that commit's message, in the revision section of the design document it names, and in the game bible (bible.html), which quotes the code's own numbers. A build is stamped on game.html (`?v=`), js/config.js (`BUILD_VERSION`) and bible.html; the letter after the date is the build of that day.

## 2026-11-18

### No build yet · branch `claude/battle-anims` · the attacks in 3D begun: the Frost Maw's three, a sample; then the fire line's ten; then the ice line's six; then the electric line's thirteen; then the toxic line's ten (behind `?engine=3d`)
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-18): "I need to begin work animating every single attack in the new battle phase"; chosen: followers and enemies; each attack its body's motion and its effect rebuilt in 3D; the Frost Maw's three first, the rest once its look is approved (`DECISION-LOG.md`).
- **What it does:** with the 3D view showing (and so in a card battle), the Frost Maw's bite, CHOMP and ABSOLUTE ZERO are drawn in 3D: his body's anticipation, strike and follow-through played smoothly from his sheet's poses, and their effects in powder snow and ice (shards, rings of snow, ice spikes, a frost haze; enemies pulled into his mouth, a freezing swirl, frost on him). The game leaves out its 2D drawing of exactly those effects. The game's rules, timings and numbers are untouched. In a battle the 2D effects were wiped while the battle's camera is up, so these are the first attack effects seen there. Every look and number the agent's, provisional (`VIEW-3D.md` §4.7).
- **The system:** a registry by form in the view (`view3d/attacks.js`), keyed by the game's own effect types, drawn from the game's state without writing it; pooled points and crystals, a budget halved on LOW graphics; a fault sends that form's effects back to 2D and the view goes on.
- `view3d/attacks.js` (new), `view3d/gameview.js`, `view3d/gamemap.js`, `tools/view3d.js`, `dist/view3d.js` (made), `js/view3d.js` (`view3dOwnsFx`), `js/elements.js` (`drawElementEffects` asks it), `js/draw.js` (the Maw's icicles), `play.html` and `dist/conduit.js` (made), `tests/view3d.js` (7 new checks, one more in the switch off), `docs/VIEW-3D.md` (§3, §4.5, §4.7, §5, §6, §7), `docs/EFFECTS.md`, `docs/CARD-BATTLES.md` §9, `docs/DECISION-LOG.md`.
- **Verified:** tests/view3d.js; the full suite (see the commit); a video of a card battle in the real page (headless, software WebGL). **Not seen on an iPad; not approved by the owner.**
- **The fire line** (the owner, 2026-11-18, approving the sample: "Wow yes! Push", then "Through the fire line."): the Quillstone's (its hit, QUILL BURST, EMBER SLASH, QUILLSTORM), the Ember Hood's (FIREBALL, HEXFIRE, METEOR RAIN) and the Thermite Burrower's (BURROW STRIKE, MAGMA VENT, FAULTLINE) attacks drawn in 3D: each body's wind-up, strike and follow-through played from its sheet's poses and the game's state, and each effect rebuilt in fire (flame tongues, embers, smoke and rubble, glowing quills, a fireball, molten rocks, spikes and craters, HEXFIRE's fissure crater, a vent, the burrower's mound, FAULTLINE's molten line). Where a 2D look was approved (the quill, the fireball's glowy blob, HEXFIRE's crater, METEOR RAIN) it is that look made 3D. The game's rules, timings and numbers are untouched. Looks and numbers the agent's, provisional (`VIEW-3D.md` §4.7).
- **The registry grew, one system still:** a fire kit (pools of flame and smoke points, solids that glow where molten, flats, ribbons; a budget halved on LOW graphics); shots in flight drawn by kind (the game leaves out its 2D drawing of a shot the view drew); a unit's own carried effect (the orbiting fireballs); effects with no maker matched by their form; a burrowing form's body kept the view's under the floor (until now it was left to the 2D, which a battle wipes, so the Burrower vanished while under).
- **Found, not changed:** in a battle EMBER SLASH's dash is pulled back towards its square by the battle's tether (`js/cardbattle.js` `_cbTether`), so the Quillstone moves only a little while its 3D streak runs the game's full line. FAULTLINE in a battle opens the path aim (the player draws it) and does not spend its charge until the path is sent; the recording drew the path as a player would. EFFECTS.md's HEXFIRE row still described the old flying orb (no orb flies since `2026-11-12h`): marked out of date.

- **The electric line** (the ice line approved, the owner, 2026-11-20: "Yes"; then "Electric"): the Thunderhead's (CHAIN LIGHTNING, STATIC VORTEX, THUNDERSTORM), the Electro Skink's (its swirl, ZIGZAG DASH, LIGHTNING CIRCUIT), the Static Orb's (its hit, SONIC ROLL, CAPACITOR DISCHARGE, OVERDRIVE) and the Hover Shell's (its hit, SHOCK ZAP, DRIFT SLASH) thirteen attacks drawn in 3D: each body's wind-up, strike and follow-through from its sheet's poses and the game's state, and each effect in lightning (jagged forking bolts along the game's own hops and routes, rings of lightning on the floor, sparks, flashes, a storm cloud, a blade of light). Where a 2D look was approved it is that look made 3D: the Thunderhead's string of lightning, STATIC VORTEX's storm (the approved model itself, No. 930, and its floor flash), the Skink's swirl (the approved model itself, No. 926). Looks approved (the owner, 2026-11-20: "Looks good", on the videos); numbers the agent's (`VIEW-3D.md` §4.7).
- **The registry grew, one system still:** a lightning pool (one draw for every bolt), a soft floor disc, the bakers' models stood in the world by an attack (made once; the storm's bands and the swirl's cells posed once behind the start screen, so posing them in play costs nearly nothing), an effect drawn while its maker's body is not (the swirl), the spark ring claimed (`js/draw.js`), the kit's pools hooked in.

The fire line's `bodyH` measures every child of a body, and for a form that keeps its glow sprites (the Thunderhead, the Static Orb) it came out NaN (the electric line measures meshes only); the Electro Skink's own body is costly to pose (the swirl's model, three of it, took 3 to 27 ms a frame posed live in the test browser; the Skink's body is posed live every frame as before); in a battle the move's `_arcPts` arc is the only record of a dash's route, so the view reads its route from it; outside the built rooms or on a fault every effect is 2D as before.

The view now draws them see-through, as the 2D does (`view3d/gameview.js`, `smokes`).

## 2026-11-17

### Build `2026-11-17ah` · branch `claude/friendly-thompson-w581x0` · card battles: each enemy's health as a callout (behind `?cards` with `?engine=3d`)
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-18): "Need to make it to where the health bars are visible somehow on the ground near each enemy"; then, of the bar laid on the floor: "a more 3D looking health bar that kind of like has a line drawn from the creature into a 2D health bar that floats atop to the side."
- **What it does:** in a battle, over each enemy, up and to its side, a raised health bar (a slanted face with a darker underside: red its health left, dark what it has lost, its numbers over it), joined to the creature by a line from a dot on it. It follows the creature; the callouts lean outwards, away from the middle of the screen.
- `js/cardbattle.js` (`_cbGroundBar`, `CARD_BATTLE.hpBar`), `tests/cardbattle.js`, `docs/CARD-BATTLES.md` §2.50, §9.
- **Verified:** tests/cardbattle.js and the full suite; seen in a real browser. **Not seen on an iPad.** Sizes the agent's.

### Build `2026-11-17ag` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · card battles: the tile attacks; walks go round everyone
- **The asks** (the owner): "We should have some cards that are single target and some cards that are tile target AoE attacks ... some might only strike in zigzag formations"; of the page of every card's shape, "Approve and push the new tile attack" (2026-11-18); and "Whenever creatures have to pass through other creatures for their attack, the game freezes up. Make sure that they avoid interactions with their teammates."
- **The tile attacks** (`CARD-BATTLES.md` §2.48): each attack card has a shape on the battle's squares, from the attack's own reach and radius (one target, a chain, a path, a zigzag, a fan, a swap, a blast, the decoy, a ring round itself). While a card is dragged its squares light up on the floor; let go (on an enemy, on a floor square for a blast or the decoy, anywhere for a ring), exactly the enemies on them take the attack's own blow and effects. A move goes off at once. MAELSTROM, HEXFIRE, MAGMA VENT, STATIC VORTEX and ROTTEN DECOY's mist stay 2 enemy turns, biting at the start of each. SWEET SCENT, SHELL GUARD and ROTTEN DECOY draw the enemies to them on the enemies' next turn. PHASE SWAP's squares change hands. CAPACITOR DISCHARGE: a full release, recharging 2 turns (the agent's, provisional). No damage number changed.
- **The freeze fixed** (§2.49): a walk went through its own side's squares, and there the squad's spacing held it 0.75 tiles short, so the battle stood still for 10 s. Every walk now goes round every taken square, and one being walked is not pushed.
- `js/cardshapes.js` (new), `js/cardbattle.js`, `js/evolution.js` (`_evoFoes`, `_evoSees`, `_evoDashOk`, `rotDecoyMist`), `js/helpers.js` (`applyDamage`), `js/clone.js` (`spawnFollowerProjectile`), `js/game.js` (a shot's own target; the spacing), `js/elements.js` (held areas), `game.html`, `tests/cardbattle.js` (16 new checks, 1 changed: 74), `docs/CARD-BATTLES.md` (§2.48, §2.49, §9, §10, §11.20 to §11.23), `docs/DECISION-LOG.md`, `docs/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`.
- **Verified:** tests/cardbattle.js and the full suite; cards dragged with the mouse in a real browser. **Not playtested; not seen on an iPad.**

- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-18): "Approve the CHOMP card and put it in."
- **What it does:** the Frost Maw's CHOMP card in the battle's pop-up shows its picture (round 2: the Maw head-on, its jaws coming down on shattering glass), as the Ember Hood's HEXFIRE does. Saved light (28 KB).

- **Verified:** tests/cardbattle.js, tests/images.js and the full suite; seen in a real browser (the Frost Maw's turn). **Not seen on an iPad.**

### Build `2026-11-17ae` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · a TANK sits out front (card battles behind `?cards` with `?engine=3d`)
- **The ask** (the owner, asked whether a TANK should stand at the front of the battle line): "Sit out front".
- **What it does** (`docs/CARD-BATTLES.md` §2.47): when a battle lines up, a follower whose play style is TANK takes the front square, before the Brawlers; then technicians, Mages and you as before. No taunt, no damage change.
- `js/cardbattle.js` (`_cbFrontFirst`), `bible.html` (the play styles' paragraph).
- **Tests:** `tests/cardbattle.js` (58 checks). **Verified before it:** the full run on `2026-11-17ad` (the merge with main's `2026-11-17aa`) passing, 132 suites. **Not playtested; not seen on an iPad.**

### Build `2026-11-17ad` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the enemies' focus: whoever has hurt them most; a TANK no longer taunts (card battles behind `?cards` with `?engine=3d`); main merged in
- **Merged with main's** `2026-11-17aa` (`claude/friendly-thompson-w581x0`: each follower attacks every turn, one follower at a time in a pop-up, HEXFIRE's card picture). This branch's rules renumbered §2.43 to §2.46 after main's §2.41 and §2.42; this branch's own `2026-11-17aa` (the TAUNT card, superseded) keeps its label in its commit.
- **The ask** (the owner): "I don't think the tank role should have automatic taunting. I think that the tank role would be best if it just did enough damage to outclass the brawler when it comes to one-on-one combat. That way, if the enemies are focused on who's doing the highest damage, then they will always choose the tank." Then: "each creature independently checks their own health for Who Is done the most damage to them, and therefore, when the technician hits for a above average normal, hit the brawler would do less damage via AOE to all the targets, therefore keeping the focus on the tank"; "Card battles only"; and "Don't change the damage numbers on any of the roles".
- **What it does** (`docs/CARD-BATTLES.md` §2.46, §9): in a battle **each creature goes for whoever has hurt it most** (its own tally of damage; none yet: whoever is in front, §2.40). **A TANK no longer draws the creatures. No play style changes damage.** The TANK's words: "DAMAGE ABSORPTION". Out of battles nothing changes.
- `js/cardbattle.js` (`cardBattleHurt`, `_cbGrudge`; the draw removed), `js/helpers.js` (`applyDamage` tells the battle each blow), `js/abilities.js` (the taunt's hook removed), `js/commandcenter.js` (the words), `bible.html` (the play styles' names and reach).
- **The agent's readings, provisional** (`CARD-BATTLES.md` §11.19): each creature's own tally (the owner's second answer; a battle total would let a Brawler's spread blows win); every hurt counts (a burn's ticks too); one it cannot reach passed over. Open: what a TANK does in a battle now.
- **Fixes:** `2026-11-17ac` renamed DEFENSIVE and AGGRESSIVE without the bible, whose number check failed; its full run was made while files were changing for this build, so it verified nothing. This build's bible is current. A first version of this build made a TANK's normal hit 1.5 times as hard; it was taken out before it was committed ("Don't change the damage numbers on any of the roles").
- **Tests:** `tests/cardbattle.js` TANKS AND THE ENEMIES' FOCUS (4 checks; 57 in all with main's), `tests/bible.js`. **Not playtested; not seen on an iPad.**

### Build `2026-11-17ac` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the play styles in a battle: CAUTIOUS and RECKLESS (behind `?cards` with `?engine=3d`; the names everywhere)
- **The ask** (the owner): "What purpose do the other roles have right now? Can we update those from a new genre of gameplay?"; then "I like the tank roll where it will play front and center there needs to be another one where it prefers to keep distance and one where it just tries to get maximum output, not giving Who or what touches it"; of what was offered, "Shoots from far", "Into the crowd", "No extra effects, none of them do anything with damage", "CAUTIOUS / RECKLESS".
- **What it does** (`docs/CARD-BATTLES.md` §2.45, §9): the roster's PLAY STYLE list is now **CAUTIOUS** ("PREFERS TO KEEP ITS DISTANCE"), BALANCED, **RECKLESS** ("MAXIMUM OUTPUT, WHATEVER TOUCHES IT") and TANK; a save keeps each follower's choice. In a battle, when a follower has to walk to attack, a CAUTIOUS one takes the farthest square it can hit from and a RECKLESS one the square next to the most enemies (BALANCED and TANK the fewest steps). No play style changes damage. Out of battles each keeps its reach as before; the mix tool's DEFEND / STEADY / PUSH are unchanged.
- `js/commandcenter.js` (`PLAY_STYLES`, the tags), `js/squadfly.js` (the tags), `js/cardbattle.js` (`_cbPickSquare`, `_cbFoesNextTo`).
- **Tests:** `tests/cardbattle.js` THE PLAY STYLES IN A BATTLE (3 checks, 56 in all); `tests/carousel.js` and `tests/ccmerge.js` (the names). **Not playtested; not seen on an iPad.**

### Build `2026-11-17ab` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · tanks: a TANK play style in the roster, in place of the TAUNT card (battles behind `?cards` with `?engine=3d`)
- **The ask** (the owner): "I think a better idea, rather than doing a taunt move card, would be maybe having a setting on each follower in the roster or under their equipment and strategy card. It should have a play style where it says like damage absorption / taunting."
- **What it does** (`docs/CARD-BATTLES.md` §2.44, §9): the roster's **PLAY STYLE** has a fourth choice, **TANK** ("DAMAGE ABSORPTION / TAUNTING"), for any follower, saved with it; the roster card's and the squad bar's tags say TANK. In a battle every creature that can reach a TANK goes for it instead of whoever is in front (the one it reaches first of two) and starts no special; the column says "LV n · TANK". Out of a battle a TANK fights at the usual reach. The TAUNT card of `2026-11-17aa` is gone (§2.43, superseded).
- `js/commandcenter.js` (`PLAY_STYLES`, the tag), `js/squadfly.js` (the tag), `js/cardbattle.js` (`_cbTank`, `_cbTaunter`; the card, its turn and its count removed).
- **The agent's readings, provisional** (`CARD-BATTLES.md` §11.19): not made tougher (the earlier "Draws attacks only"); always on while set; nobody is a TANK until set, technicians included.
- **Tests:** `tests/cardbattle.js` TANKS (4 checks, 53 in all), `tests/carousel.js` (the PLAY STYLE list with TANK). **Not playtested; not seen on an iPad.**

### Build `2026-11-17aa` (this branch's; main's `2026-11-17aa` below is the card-battle agent's) · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · card battles: tanks, the technician's TAUNT (behind `?cards` with `?engine=3d`)
- **The ask** (the owner): "add a tank role that draws attacks"; asked who, how and how tough: "The technicians are built as a tank, but you should just make it to where whoever's right in front of the creature is who it attacks at the beginning. I don't know I mean, we could think we could think this through because I'm not quite sure."; "When it taunts"; "Draws attacks only".
- **What it does** (`docs/CARD-BATTLES.md` §2.43, §9): every technician has a **TAUNT** card (TANK). Playing it is its turn, and for the enemies' next 2 turns every creature that can reach it goes for it (bites it, or walks towards it) and starts no special. It is not made tougher. ### Build `2026-11-17k` (stamped `2026-11-17e` on its branch; main had taken e to j) · branch `claude/card-artist` · the Frost Maw is powder snow
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-17, of the Frost Maw on a draft attack card: "it's hard to tell what he is"): "He's supposed to have two icicles fangs and their within his Pac-Man looking body but it's made of powdered snow ... but instead right now it looks like it's a crystaline looking icy exterior". Of a first draft (smooth, a round mouth, icicle fangs): **"No, I like his old teeth and the powder material needs more of a snowy texture."** Of the second: **"shouldn't be able to see snow inside of his mouth in the back, but other than that, it looks absolutely awesome"**, then, circling the two points low at each side of the mouth: **"Get rid of the side teeth somehow."** Of the third: **"Frost maw looks good"**.
- **What changed** (`design/evolutions/forms10.js`: the new model is No. 947, `_powderDome`; No. 934 kept as its history; `design/evolutions/bake.js` bakes `maw` from No. 947): his body is powder snow, soft clumps with smaller lumps and faint blue in the hollows, built into the shape (not a picture laid on it), instead of flat facets of different brightness. His shape, size, colours, the jagged teeth along the top of his mouth and his two crystal fangs are as they were. The two points low at each side of his mouth are gone (the sides and bottom of the mouth's edge are round). The snow heaped round his foot and the chunks on the floor sit outside his body, so none shows inside his mouth.
- **His sheets** (`img/evo/maw.png`, `maw_g.png`) baked again, all 8 facings and 32 frames (idle, the lunge-bite, CHOMP), cropped (`js/evopack.js`); his Bible picture (`img/bible/forms/maw.webp`) made again. His moves, timings and numbers are unchanged.
- **Found and fixed on the way:** the Bible's form pictures (`design/bible/pictures.js forms`) cut a form's first cell from the top-left corner of its sheet, which on a cropped sheet (since build `2026-11-09r`) is a jumble of packed cells. It now finds the cell in `js/evopack.js`, as the game does; `FORMS=maw` makes only the forms named. Only the Frost Maw's picture was made again (the others, made before the sheets were cropped, are right).
- **Known, not changed:** 29 of his 256 frames (his biggest lunge and CHOMP poses) touch the edge of their 192 px cell; the old model's did the same 29.
- Verified: the full test run (see the commit); the new Frost Maw in the real game page (standing, and swallowing with ABSOLUTE ZERO), its picture right after the picture tool's WebP copy was made. **Not playtested on an iPad.**

As the 2D draws them (`js/facilitysewers.js`): the **water** (the river, the reservoir, the moat) sunk 12 px under the floor, its streaks drifting the way it flows, in the 2D's toxic green that clears as the purification runs; the **banks** down to it, a hazard stripe along the ledges' lips, and along the dam its face of compacted trash with the **rim** of junk on it; the bridges' **gratings** and rails, the broken bridge's stumps, the **gates'** bars, the **pumps** and **valves**; the Nest's **eggs**, glowing as the Nest stands (dark once cleared), and the dam's **junk**; the **big barred arch** in the north wall with the stream out under its bars into the reservoir; the **brood holes** with the brood's green deep in them, the colony's **resin**, the **outfall's** violet pour, the **sealed hatch**; the **spill lips** with their faces down into the dark and the **waterfall** over the east one, light at the lip, fading as it falls into its pit. ### Build `2026-11-17d` · branch `claude/friendly-thompson-w581x0` · card battles, step 1: a battle starts and looks like one (behind `?cards` with `?engine=3d`)
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-17: turn-based card battles, `CARD-BATTLES.md`; then "Build it": the first playable step, in the 3D game behind a switch).
- **What it does, only with `?cards` and `?engine=3d`:** a tap on a creature, or a creature's hit on you or your squad, starts a battle with it and the creatures round it. A far creature: you walk towards it, the screen goes black, and you and your squad land in front of it in its sight. The camera eases to the fight, lower, turned to the side and nearer; the edges blur and darken; the bars and labels over the picture wait. Creatures not in it walk past; your squad goes only for those in it. RUN (a button for now) ends it. **No cards or turns yet**: inside the battle the fight is the real-time one. Without the switch nothing changes.
- `js/cardbattle.js` (new), `js/targeting.js`, `js/helpers.js`, `js/predator.js`, `js/npc.js`, `js/game.js`, `js/battleview.js`, `view3d/gameview.js`, `view3d/gamemap.js`, `game.html`, `tests/cardbattle.js` (new), `docs/CARD-BATTLES.md` §9 and §10, `docs/VIEW-3D.md`.
- **Verified:** tests/cardbattle.js and the full suite; seen in a headless browser with software WebGL. **Not playtested; not seen on an iPad.** Every number (what is far, the walk, the black, the camera's angle and zoom) is the agent's, provisional.

Coming down the lab's stairwell you arrive at the stairs' foot. The Lab Relay powers both; the network counts 38 rooms. The layout and the things in it are the agent's, provisional. The freezer bank, the toppled racks and the intact part's things are their own models. The cave's and the tunnel's rock is the arena's layered rock in the room's own blues (half its walls', half its floor's), frost-white on top, its pits a cold blue-grey with a little sheen, icicles under the bits that jut out; the cave's and the tunnel's floors have a sheet of cyan ice (the look only). ### Build `2026-11-17d` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the game in 3D: a Thunderhead no longer turns the 3D view off
- **The report** (the owner): **"it only switches back when I have a certain follower, and it doesn't switch back to 3D now that I have the follower"** … **"It's the thungerhead btw"**. Reproduced: a Thunderhead in the room, and on the first frame the view failed ("Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'material')") and the game went to 2D for the rest of the game, as a failure does.
- **The cause** (`view3d/core.js`): building a form's body, the view merges the parts that never move into one shape, and took the merged parts out of the model. The Thunderhead's shards fade their glowing edges each frame, finding the edge as its group's second part; with the shard's core merged and taken out, the edge was the first, and there was no second. **Fixed:** a form's merged parts stay where they were, hidden (a room's are still taken out, to keep the iPad's memory).
- **And** (`view3d/gameview.js`): a follower whose body fails to build or pose is drawn by the game in 2D, that form alone, and the view goes on; one form's fault no longer turns the whole view off.
- **Tests**: `tests/view3d.js`: every form and egg (40) built as a follower's body is and put in every pose the game can ask of it (its idle, action and block columns), with no error; a Thunderhead beside you drawn by the view, the view still on. On the code before the fix the check names the Thunderhead with that error.
- **Verified:** the full run, all 129 suites passing; a picture of the Thunderhead in the 3D room, beside its 2D sheet (the same dark cloud, black shards and yellow glints). **Not tried on an iPad.**

### Build `2026-11-17c` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the game in 3D: one finger swiped turns the room
- **The ask** (the owner): **"let's just do it where it's one finger will allow you to rotate the camera. And you swipe it. You swipe whichever direction you want to go. Or opposite, however it's working now."**
- **Now** (`js/view3d.js`; `docs/VIEW-3D.md` §4.1): on a press the game leaves plain (not on a creature or its card's buttons, the gun's shot, the dialogue's box, an aim or a ring), a finger gone 22 px more sideways than up or down turns the room with it, 0.25° a px, like a turntable (the floor in front goes the way the finger goes); meanwhile the 2D over it waits, as in a swing. Let go, it settles on a quarter turn: the next one past 20°, or one on if it was flicked, else back. A tap is still a tap (it walks), a hold still opens the ring, up and down does nothing new. **Two fingers only zoom now** (the twist of `2026-11-17b` is gone). On a computer a mouse drag sideways turns the same way. 22 px, 0.25°, 20° and the flick's speed (0.5 px a ms) are the agent's, provisional; the direction can be turned round.
- A first finger down clears what was kept of fingers whose lift never came, so a lost lift cannot make a later finger look like a second one.
- **Tests**: `tests/view3d.js` (a 10 px drag, a slow 150 px swipe right, 64 px left, a 40 px flick, up and down, a swipe on a creature; a pinch with a twist in it zooms and does not turn). Real touches in Chromium (a probe, not in the run): a swipe right and back turn and settle, up and down and a hold's drag do not turn, a pinch zooms.
- **Verified:** the full run, all 129 suites passing; pictures from the real page mid-swipe and settled. **Not tried on an iPad.**

### Build `2026-11-17a` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the game in 3D: the camera turns a quarter at a time and zooms
- **The ask** (the owner, of what next): **"there was so much more in the demo than there is now there was a one key thing of note is the fact that I could rotate the camera and zoom in zoom out"**; asked how to turn, with the 2D still drawn over the picture laid out for one angle: **"90° steps"**.
- **Turning** (`docs/VIEW-3D.md` §4.1): a quarter at a time, the camera swinging there in 0.4 s. Everything the game lays out on the screen from the world now goes through one turn (`js/config.js` `VIEW_TURN`, `isoSX`, `isoSY`, `isoDX`, `isoDY`, `isoBack`, `isoDepth`: 114 lines across 28 files that worked out the screen by the one angle's formula; the facility's own `_fpP`): bars, plates, numbers, effects, the floor's marks, taps, the order of drawing, the 2D creatures' facing. While it swings the 2D over the picture waits. The walls that sink are those in front of your room as the turned camera sees them (each wall piece knows its rooms for each turn).
- **Zoom**, 0.6 to 1.8 times: the camera and the world's 2D drawing scaled about the screen's middle together; taps come back through it; zoomed out, more of the world is drawn.
- **Controls**: four round buttons under QUESTS (turn left, turn right, zoom out, zoom in); on an iPad a pinch zooms and a two-finger twist turns; on a computer the wheel zooms, `[` `]` turn, `-` `=` zoom. Only while the 3D view shows; the 2D game keeps its one angle and scale. While the dialogue's box is up the buttons are under it; with a panel open (the pack, the clone bay, the armoury, the quest box, INFO, Settings) they are not drawn over it, nor take its taps, and fingers, the wheel and the keys leave the camera alone. Turned, the drillable rubble is drawn as its drawn pile.
- **Tests**: `tests/view3d.js` (in three turns and three zooms the view and the game's own layout match a turned projection to the pixel; a tap comes back to its tile; nothing standing hides anyone in any of the four turns); `tests/battlelook.js`, `tests/weapon.js` follow the change (the network panel held back while the 3D view shows; the weapon's tap through the turn).
- **Verified:** the full run, all 129 suites passing (before the buttons stood aside for the dialogue's box and the panels; after it `view3d`, `bundle` and `battlelook`, with a new check that fails without it); pictures from the real page, the buttons clicked, the wheel and the keys. **Not seen on an iPad; the pinch and twist not tried on a touch screen.**

### Build `2026-11-16a` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the real game in 3D, stage 1: the lab's ground floor (behind `?engine=3d`)
- **The ask** (the owner, having tried the test page: "Can we build the full game up to this point?"; asked which way): **"The real game in 3D"**: the actual game drawn in the chosen look, built area by area, behind a switch while it grows; its rules, squad, creatures, cards and saves as they are.

The camera is the game's own, so the bars, plates, effects, panels and taps lie where they always did. The floor's play marks (pools, fire, auras, the long-press ring) are laid on the 3D floor.

- **One thing moves:** what is drawn over a unit (its bars, its plate) is drawn half a tile lower while the 3D view shows, where the body stands (the 2D drawing put a unit's feet at its tile's top corner).

- **Verified:** `tests/view3d.js` (14 checks: the files; off, nothing of it; on, made with no error and showing; the camera the game's projection to the pixel on the floor and the stairs; the bodies; doors, light, blackout, floor marks, the items kept; away in the arena in 2D; a failure leaves the game in 2D), `tests/lab3d.js` (21) and the full suite; pictures from the real page in every room. **Not seen on an iPad.**

### A test page, no change to the game · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` (on build `2026-11-15j`) · the test page: the camera always on the cube, and no model flattened

### A test page, no change to the game · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` (on build `2026-11-15j`) · the test page: walk the lab floor, a fight at a pace you set, a battle camera
- **The ask** (the owner, having read 52 fps on an iPad: "Wow, that's exciting. I'd love to see more"; of three things offered next, all three): "Walk the lab floor", "A fight at the new pace", "A battle camera".
- **Walk the lab floor** (`docs/STYLE-EVOLUTION.md` §8.2): the page is now the lab's whole ground floor in look 1, eight rooms from the game's map and prop models. Tap the floor and the cube walks there; the squad follows; the camera follows; the walls in front of the room you are in sink into the floor.
- **A fight at the new pace** (§8.3): ants come up in waves for the Crystal and the squad and the sentries fight them. Every blow is marked on the floor before it lands (a wedge, a disc, a strip or a dashed line, filling), and lands on whoever stands in the shape then. PACE sets the fight's speed (×1, ×0.8, ×0.6, ×0.4). **A test of pace and signals:** the ants' speeds, health and power and the bite's two beats are the game's; every other number is a stand-in, and none is a decision about the game.
- **A battle camera** (§8.4): CAMERA AUTO comes down close to a fight and goes back out after it; NEAR and FAR hold it. *(AUTO taken out the same day: the entry above.)*
- **The glow round the Crystal** drew as a ring of dots on the owner's iPad. The picture is now drawn in the colours the screen shows, and the glow's disc is made from numbers, not a canvas gradient. Not yet seen on an iPad.
- **Cost** (§8.6): about 390 draws and 600,000 triangles a frame at the Crystal Chamber with the shadows' drawing, about 290 and 355,000 on the frames between (the first room alone: 395 and 397,000). Each room has one plain copy for the ink line's and the shadows' drawings; the shadows are drawn every other frame.
- **Round 2's room builder** (`design/style-evolution/round2/chamber.js`) can now build any room; the ten still pictures render byte for byte as before. Its exporter gained `--lab`.
- **Verified:** `tests/lab3d.js` (18 checks: the page; apart from the game; it runs, with WebGL 1 too; the switches; a finger and a tap; the floor; the walk through four rooms; the walls; a whole raid with every blow marked for its whole wind-up; the pace; the camera; raids off), and the full suite. **Not measured on an iPad as it is now. Whether a pace reads well is for the owner's eye.**

### Build `2026-11-15j` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the Sewers' gates: drawn behind whoever stands in front of them
- **The ask** (the owner: "Fix the gate"): after `2026-11-15h`'s pictures, where the player stood in front of the wide gate to Side Quest's platform and its bars were drawn over them.
- **Why:** each gate was one item, sorted as a whole by its far (east) end. Anyone in front of the rest of it was sorted before it, so the bars covered them.
- **What it does** (`docs/FACILITY.md`, "The Sewers"): a gate is drawn in pieces two bars wide, cut between the bars, each sorted by its own far end, as the walls' pieces are. Whoever stands in front of a gate is drawn over it, and whoever stands behind it (Side Quest on his platform) under it. The chain and padlock are an item of their own, sorted just after the pieces under them. The look is unchanged (the gate drawn this way is within a few faint pixels of the old one, at the seams). Both gates cost 122 canvas calls a frame against 106.
- `js/facilitysewers.js`, `tests/sewerlook.js` (two new checks: the pieces and their sorting on every tile along both gates, and on the real page the player in front of the wide gate drawn over it and a follower behind it drawn under it; the first failed before the fix), `tests/sewers.js` (gates counted by name, not by item), `docs/FACILITY.md`.
- **Also on this branch:** main's `2026-11-15i` (METEOR RAIN), taken in first.
- **Verified:** `tests/sewerlook.js`, `tests/sewers.js` and the suites near them, then the full suite; pictures of the player in front of the gate and of Side Quest behind it, no page errors. **Not playtested on an iPad.**

### Build `2026-11-15i` (stamped `2026-11-15g` on its branch; main had taken g and h) · branch `claude/meteor-look` · METEOR RAIN: the Ember Hood's new Ultimate (VOLCANIC ERUPTION is superseded)
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-15, going through a video of every Ultimate): of VOLCANIC ERUPTION, "I don't like it. It was created by and designed by AI. Let's brainstorm some new directions."; of six directions, "3" (meteor rain); of a draft over the real page, "Change the craters to 3d art", then "Trust your instincts and fix the 3D result the way you'd want it"; of the result, **"I like it"**. The rules, chosen from options: the rocks land **"All random"**; the craters are **for show only**; the strength is the **"Same total"** as the eruption's; the name is **METEOR RAIN**.
- **What it does** (`docs/COMBAT.md`, "METEOR RAIN"; `js/elements.js` `ULT_METEOR`, `ULT_METEOR_RAIN`, `meteorRainTick`): it goes for the biggest enemy within 12 tiles, as the eruption did. The Hood blazes (its HEXFIRE cast frames) for 40 frames, then 14 burning rocks fall one after another over about 2.7 s, each at a random spot within 3.2 tiles of where that enemy stood that can be seen from there, no two within 0.9 tiles. A red mark closes in on the floor for half a second before each lands. A rock hurts every enemy within 1.5 tiles of where it lands that can be seen from there: 6.5 × the Hood's special attack (fire, a special hit) and a burn (3 s). The crater it leaves hurts nothing and is gone in under 4 s. A rain in a small room has fewer rocks. With no enemy in reach it keeps its charge; a second cannot start while the first falls.
- **How it looks** (`docs/EFFECTS.md`): the rock and the crater are **3-D modelled pieces** (`meteorrock`, `meteorcrater`: design/effects/bake_fx.js, js/fx3d.js), approved, so on for every player; the rock's tail, the marks, the strike's flash, the rubble and the flames on a hot crater are drawn in flat colours. The craters are drawn in the floor's pass, so the walls and the units cover them. Until a sheet has loaded, flat looks stand in.
- **Left out of the draft:** the room going dark and red while the rain falls. The effects plan allows no full-screen fill (it was the blizzard's fault once: tests/effects.js), so the build has none.
- **The strength, measured:** against three still ants the eruption dealt 464 in all (its hit, its long burn and its crater's burn, which a still enemy takes in full). The rain at 6.5 × averages about the same over 60 rains (a scratch measure, not in the test run). **It swings**: with every rock at random a rain lands between two and nine hits on the three (five on average), and about one enemy in fifteen is missed altogether.
- **Kept, unused:** the eruption's own code (`ULT_VOLCANIC_ERUPTION`, `activeFireEruption`, its column and crater) is still in js/elements.js; nothing fires it. The Ultimate's icon on the party card is a falling rock now.
- **Agent's, provisional:** every number above but the owner's four choices (the count, the area, the blast, the spacing, the timings, 6.5 ×, the burn); the icon.
- Tests: followercombat (METEOR RAIN, new; OWN ULTIMATES), foundation, fx3d (the two pieces, new). The full run: see the commit. Pictures and a video from the real page. **Not playtested on an iPad.**
### Build `2026-11-15h` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the Sewers: no dark block over the river by the outfall
- **The report** (the owner, circling it on a picture of `2026-11-15g`): "I see this error": a dark block standing over the river's west end, beside the outfall pipe.
- **Why:** the new look cuts every near wall clean and hangs its face below the floor. The south side of the little alcove beside the outfall is a near wall, but it is the end of the west wall itself, so its face hung where the river is, and the river is drawn before everything. The same thing happened, hidden, at the two corners where the south wall meets the subway passage and the Nest.

### Build `2026-11-15b` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the floor painted once: less for the iPad to paint each frame
- **The report** (the owner, 2026-11-15, a screenshot of Settings → PERFORMANCE on the iPad, Main Hallway, build `2026-11-15a`, graphics LOW): **FPS 27**, frame 37 ms = logic 0.6 + draw 8.6 + catch-up 2.1 + **browser 25.5**; gaps between frames steady at 38 to 48 ms.
  - The game's own work was 11 ms of each frame. The rest was the browser painting what the game drew.
- **Found** (headless Chromium, the same spot at the iPad's size, 1366 by 892, graphics LOW): the canvas was asked for **4,021 operations a frame**. That included 941 pictures covering 7.3 M pixels (six times the screen) and 415 filled shapes, 244 of them drawn with additive light.
  - The page's own controls changed about once a frame and only by writing the same value again, and the CORE cube's canvas did 18 operations. So the cost is the game's canvas.
  - **The floor was painted twice.** Every tile drew the game's own tile (447 pictures, 3.4 M pixels), then the facility's tile over it, whose opaque base covers it whole.
  - **Every flickering pool of light** (the lamps' light on the floor) was a shape built and filled each frame, tile by tile.
- **Fixed** (the same look):
  - **The game's own tile is left out under a facility tile with an opaque base** (js/game.js; js/facilitydecor.js `facilityFloorHides`), once that tile's picture is made. The bridge's grating and the fill cells under the walls are as before.
  - **A flickering light is stamped** from a diamond painted once per colour, at its strength this frame (`_fpGlowPic`). Through the dimmed pen it is filled as before, since the pen darkens a colour and never a picture.
- **After** (the same spot): **2,110 operations a frame** (48% fewer), 738 pictures covering 5.7 M pixels, 171 shapes filled.
- **The look:** the same frames drawn the new and the old way in 15 rooms differ by at most 13 levels (of 255), along the seams between floor tiles and inside the pools of light. The seams are a hair lighter (44,324 pixels lighter, 4,555 darker in the Main Hallway), because the old dark tile no longer shows through them.
- **Tests:** `tests/floorpaint.js` (new) checks the wiring, the Main Hallway and the arena (the game's tile left out under all 474 and 379 tiles, the lights stamped, the frame within 16 levels of the old), and the dimmed pen. `tests/run.js`.
- `js/game.js`, `js/facilitydecor.js`, `tests/floorpaint.js`, `tests/run.js`, `docs/TUTORIAL.md` (Frame cost).
- **Not measured on an iPad.** How much faster the iPad paints is not known until the readout is taken again there.

### Build `2026-11-15a` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the pictures kept to a budget: no slowdown after minutes of play
- **The report** (the owner, 2026-11-15): "So every single time I be playing my game, it starts lagging, or so the frame rate dips to about half after like 5 minutes of play. Can we make it to where it doesn't do that and loads new assets correctly? Or I don't think it's because I'm on the iPad."
- **Found.** The test: headless Chromium, the player taken to a new room every 8 seconds, every canvas the game made tracked while it lived, counted after a full garbage collection. The game kept a picture of every floor tile it had ever drawn. For the props it kept every slice, a silhouette of each slice, and a dimmed copy for every light level a prop was ever seen at. None was ever let go. After 3.5 minutes that was **380 MB of canvases, still climbing** (floor tiles 137 MB, dimmed props 134 MB, slices and their silhouettes 50 MB). The creatures, the script's own memory (about 30 MB) and the followers stayed steady.
  - On an iPad, the more canvas memory the browser holds, the slower it draws. Past iPadOS's limit (a few hundred MB) it can make no new canvas, which likely explains pictures not loading.
  - So it is not the iPad: the same climb happens on any device, and the iPad feels it first.
- **Fixed** (js/facilitydecor.js `FP_FLOOR_SPR`, js/propsprites.js `PS_MEM`). Each picture is counted as it is made and stamped with the frame it is drawn in. Past a budget, those least recently drawn are let go, their memory released at once, down to three quarters of the budget. One drawn this frame is never let go, and one let go is made again, the same picture, the next time it is on the screen. The rock walls' pictures already worked this way (build `2026-11-12c`).
  - **Budgets** (the agent's, provisional): floor 6 M pixels (about 24 MB), props 12 M (about 48 MB). On a bigger screen they grow to 6 and 12 times the screen's pixels, so one screen's pictures always fit.
  - **A dimmed copy now carries its own silhouette** (made on its own canvas, the same pixels), so no silhouette is kept beside each slice and prop sheet. This saves a third of a dim room's prop memory: the arena's props take 4.7 M pixels once its light has held, down from 7 M.
  - **A light level not drawn for a second is let go** (`PS_MEM.dimHold`), so a light that changed or came on leaves no copies behind.
- **After** (the same exploration, 4.5 minutes): canvas memory levels off at **100 to 116 MB**. That is rock 31 MB (its old budget), floor 19, dimmed props 15, slices 14, the battle view's layers 11.
  - The cost: a room seen again after its pictures were let go has them made again, as on its first visit.
- **The look is unchanged.** The same frames drawn by the old and the new code matched pixel for pixel in 12 rooms, with the units left out of the shot. In two runs, under 30 pixels differed in one or two rooms, where two frames of the same code also differed (scene noise).
- **Tests:** `tests/picmem.js` (new) checks the counts, the same picture after everything is let go, the dimmed copies against the old way, a level let go after a second, the budgets on a big screen, and a tour of every room within the budgets with no room making pictures again on its second frame. `tests/ghost.js` allows the new drawing state. `tests/facilityplay.js` no longer stubs the removed silhouette sheets.
  - **The first full run:** 123 of 124 suites passed. The one failure was `tests/facilityplay.js`'s check that no follower stalls on its way to you (one follower stalled in one 40-frame window). That check draws nothing, and its followers move by chance that is not seeded. It passed 11 times after, alone and four at a time, and in the 11 full runs before this build.
- `js/facilitydecor.js`, `js/propsprites.js`, `tests/picmem.js`, `tests/run.js`, `tests/ghost.js`, `tests/facilityplay.js`, `docs/TUTORIAL.md` (Frame cost), `docs/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md` (a pointer for the modelled walls).
- **Not measured on an iPad; not playtested.** The 30 MB of rock pictures and the units' cached cells (`spriteCell`, 12 M pixels at most) keep their old budgets.

### Build `2026-11-14t` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · a refresh keeps the Ultimates
- **The report** (the owner, 2026-11-14): "Refreshing the game resets my followers' ultimates."
- **The cause:** the save kept each follower's level, XP, evolution, moves and cards but not its Ultimate's charge, and a follower made from the save had none at all (its meter hidden until it charged again). Your own ULTIMATE bar on THE CORE was not kept either: a refresh emptied it too.
- **Now:** each follower's record keeps its charge (`ult`, to a tenth, never rounded up to full) and the follower made from it has it; your bar is kept (`pult`). A game saved before has none: empty, as before. A fallen follower's is not kept (its return starts it empty, as a fall always did); a bad value loads within 0 to 100.

### Build `2026-11-14s` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · VOLTWYRM's fight, diagnosed: a lighter battle view, steady facings
- **Asked** (the owner, 2026-11-14): "The game runs pretty slow, or feels like it when I'm in the electrical domain fighting the voltwyrm. Also its movement and the movement of the followers looks glitched when they flip back and forth with their direction. Diagnostics are required".
- **The diagnostics** (the real page in desktop Chrome at 1180×820, the size an iPad Air draws; each part of the frame timed, a CPU profile, every unit's facing each frame): (1) in any fight the battle view's copies of the screen were most of the frame on HIGH (about 21 ms a picture in VOLTWYRM's fight: 31 pictures a second), and a step with no picture (a device catching up) made them all again, so a fight that fell behind copied twice; (2) VOLTWYRM picks whom to bite five times a second, and whoever hit it last counts for more than keeping its target, so with two brawlers hitting it in turn it turned between them every 10 to 20 frames: its heading wavered from 16 to 32 degrees, across the line at 22.5 between two of its eight pictures, and the whole picture flipped 45 degrees back and forth; (3) its brawlers stood 1.4 tiles from its middle, inside its body (1.8 round), packed and jostling, and a follower thrown by SHOCK RING turned to face the throw, then back.
- **Lighter** (the same look): on HIGH the battle view builds its blur, mask and darkening as one layer at half the screen's size and lays it on once (the same picture: at most a few levels apart on a few pixels in 10,000; 27.6 → 11.9 ms in the browser test); a step with no picture makes no copies; VOLTWYRM and the roly-polies are drawn from small cells cut once, as every form is since build `2026-11-09d`.
- **Steady:** a picture of eight facings (an evolved form, VOLTWYRM, a roly-poly) keeps its facing until the heading is about 12 degrees past the line to the next (`js/draw.js` `faceEighth`); a follower within 2.5 tiles of the edge of what it fights faces it, thrown back it keeps facing as it was, and a jostle under a third of its step does not turn it; a Brawler strikes a huge body at its edge (VOLTWYRM 2.0 from its middle; the queen 1.4, as before). The numbers are the agent's, provisional.
- **Measured** (before → after, two runs each): VOLTWYRM's fight on HIGH 31 → 38 to 45 pictures a second, on LOW about 48 either way; an ant fight in the arena on HIGH 21 → 23.5; VOLTWYRM's facing flips 8 and 4 → 0; its followers' facing changes 13 and 7 → 1. In the ant fight the followers now turn to the ant they hit (a turn every 20 to 30 frames, none flipping back).
- **Not changed, the owner's call** (`OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`): whom VOLTWYRM bites. It still turns from attacker to attacker; should a boss keep its target longer?
- **Also:** the ghost test's list of drawing-only state lacked SEEDFALL's and HEXFIRE's caches (`_bdFrame`, `_hxFrame`, since 2026-11-12), so it failed on and off, when they were drawn: added. And the frame budget's 22,000 canvas operations sat inside its scene's own noise (21,744 to 22,194 across runs; the code before measured 21,811 to 21,939): 22,500 (`tests/perf.js`). And, merging main: build `2026-11-14o`'s check that an icicle never freezes a boss (`tests/evolution.js`) passed by luck: off the map, where its scene stands, nothing has a floor, so every icicle stopped on its first step, and the boss was hurt only when a Brawler left by the checks before struck it that frame (on and off, 2 runs in 12). In its open field shots now fly as sight already did, only the Rimestar and the boss are in the frame, and the check asks that the icicles slowed the boss (a boss is slowed, not frozen).
- `js/battleview.js`, `js/draw.js`, `js/larva.js`, `js/rolypoly.js`, `js/evolution.js`, `js/npc.js` (`NPC_FACE`, the Brawler's reach); `tests/larva.js` (4 checks), `tests/battlelook.js`, `tests/rolypoly.js`, `tests/ghost.js`; `COMBAT.md`, `BATTLE-LOOK.md`, `ELECTRIC-BREED.md` §10.
- Not measured on an iPad (GRAPHICS: LOW skips the blur; Settings' PERFORMANCE readout shows a frame's logic, drawing and catch-up there). Not playtested.

### Build `2026-11-14o` (stamped 14n on its branch) · branch `claude/balance-rerun-2` · the first balance pass: four moves brought down
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-14: "List ways to nerf each one"; of the agent's pick for each, **"Go with your picks"**).
- **ERUPTION** (Seeper): 1.8 → **1.2 ×** its attack (its area and its armour break as they were). Score 149.4 → 99.6.
- **SKATE CHARGE** (Igloo Skater): 1 → **0.65 ×** (its slow as it was). 68.5 → 47.1.
- **SONIC ROLL** (Static Orb): its chain 60% → **40%** of the hit a jump (the first hit, 2.4 ×, as it was). 120.3 → 100.5.
- **BLADE ROLL** (Hammer Slug): 1.2 → **1 ×**. 51.6 → 43.0, in its band.
- **Left as they were:** STAKE RING (46.7) and MAELSTROM (53.3), barely over their bands.
- **After the pass** (the trial run again, the neutral dummy): the bands are Brawler 59.4 to 99, Mage 32.5 to 54.2, Technician 26.2 to 43.7. ERUPTION and SONIC ROLL sit at the top edge of the Brawler band (1.3 × the target), SKATE CHARGE and STAKE RING a little over the Technician band. Still below: Hornshell 44%, SUNDEW 48%, Rimestar 55%, Goo Proxy 57%, Frost Maw and Hover Shell 72%.

### Build `2026-11-14n` (stamped 14m on its branch) · branch `claude/balance-rerun-2` · the balance trial fights a neutral dummy; a column for the ants
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-14: "Rerun the trials"; "What should we do to balance"; of the agent's first step, **"Yes, do the neutral dummy"**).
- **Why:** the trial fought ants only, and an ant is weak to Crushing and Earthquake and resists Elemental, so the scores mixed the attacks' own worth with the ant match-up.
- **The scored pass fights dummies with no weakness and no resistance** (`tools/balance-trial.js`: the enemy has no race's row); the bands on `balance.html` are made from it. **A second pass fights ants**: each mode's "Damage vs ants" and "Ants ×" (how much the ants add or take), and each form's "vs ants" score.
- **What it shows:** against ants every form deals less (× 0.74 to 0.97): the Elemental half of every hit is resisted, and the attacks typed Crushing or Earthquake lose least (ERUPTION × 0.97, GRAVITY SLAM × 0.94), those typed Elemental most (the Thunderhead, the Voidstar, the Ember Hood × 0.74 to 0.75). The flags hold on the neutral dummy: the Igloo Skater's SKATE CHARGE 2 × its target, ERUPTION 1.9 ×, SONIC ROLL 1.5 ×; the Hornshell and SUNDEW at 46%.
- Run on the game of build `2026-11-14l` (no perks, no traits). The game itself is not changed by this build.
- `tools/balance-trial.js`, `tools/balance.js`, `design/balance-trial.json`, `balance.html`.

### Build `2026-11-14m` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · VOLTWYRM redesigned: a clawed tail, strut legs, a more angular head
- **Asked** (the owner, 2026-11-14): "I want to redesign the volt worm, and I want to make the tail a little bit thicker and wider, and make it have like claws on the end, like Groudon from Pokemon"; of the drafts: "A and make the tail and body need more plating on each segment", then "I like a, now legs should be more angular at least have single claw feet", then, circling leg draft 3, "I like this one and can you make the head slightly more angular and that's perfect".
- **The tail** (draft A, in place of the twin prongs): thicker and wider toward its end, an armoured wrist lit round its rim, and three heavy pale talons splayed like a raptor's foot, hooking down to the floor, their inner edges lit.
- **The legs** (draft 3, in place of the paddle legs): up from the hip, out level and down in hard bends; flat-sided struts with an armour plate on top, pistons across the knee, light in every joint, and one hooked claw on each foot, pale like its jaws, its inner edge lit. They step in the same two tripods.
- **The head**, a little more angular: its outline cut in straight facets to a prow, its edges chamfered flat, the crest and the plates beside it wedges pointing forward, hexagonal lens cups, faceted horns with diamond tips. Its jaws and lenses as before. No extra plating ("I like a" read as tail A as drafted).
- The claws keep their own light while SICKLE SNAP's warning lights the jaws. Lying across the screen it is 396 px long (377 before), about 4.7 tiles from jaws to talons; its rules, its size number and its body's reach are unchanged.
- `design/props/models/larva.js` (its `LOOK`; the drafts stay as options for `design/creatures/voltwyrm_tail_studies.js`, which draws all four rounds), `img/props/larva.png` and `js/propatlas.js` re-baked, the Bible's picture.

### Build `2026-11-14l` (stamped 14f on its branch) · branch `claude/remove-perks` · the followers' four traits are gone
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-14: "Can you give me a rundown on the traits and if they should stick around or not?"; after the rundown, **"Remove all four"**).
- **What they were:** every follower drew one combat trait and one natural trait at random when it was made, shown only on its INFO card: **BERSERKER** (its hits × 1.5 under 30% health), **OPPORTUNISTIC** (it went for the lowest-health enemy within 6 tiles, not the nearest), **EMPATHETIC** (a quarter nearer you in the formation) and **LONE WOLF** (1.2 tiles farther out).
- **Now:** `COMBAT_TRAITS` and `NATURAL_TRAITS` (js/traits.js) are empty. No follower draws one; a saved one is dropped on load and on respawn; what a trait had left set on a follower (its multiplier, its distance) is cleared. Every follower goes for the nearest enemy (an attack order and FOCUS as before), hits alike at any health, and stands in its formation place. The INFO card has no TRAITS section.
- **What changes in play:** about half the followers lose a 50% damage boost when near death; about half no longer chase the weakest enemy past nearer ones; places in the formation shift by up to about a tile.
- `js/traits.js`, `js/helpers.js`, `js/npc.js`, `js/facilitysave.js`, `js/game.js`, `js/infodex.js`, `tests/traits.js` (none exists or is drawn, a saved one comes back as none, each of the four does nothing), `tests/formation.js`, `tests/unitinfo.js`, `docs/COMBAT.md`, the Bible, `docs/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`, `docs/DECISION-LOG.md`.
- Not playtested. The balance trial was not run again.

### Build `2026-11-14j` (stamped 14d on its branch) · branch `claude/balance-rerun` · the balance page counts the statuses a form distributes; a lure is no longer counted twice
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-14: "Rerun tests"; then "Are we accounting for support points? I feel like the Rotten Decoy would do a lot of support if it's holding down enemies, and I don't understand how the Hammer Slug's shell guard is at 1.9 above their band"; told how the score was made, **"I think you should count the statuses that are distributed"**).
- **What was wrong:** SHELL GUARD scored 63.5 from a lure counted as the enemy's whole damage held off (though its hits still land on the Slug) plus the damage its guard cut, counted before defence; and the damage one enemy deals, taken from a live run, moved between 5 and 12 from one run to the next, so every support score moved with it.
- **Support is now the statuses a form puts on enemies**, each tallied apart by the trial (enemy-seconds stunned, glitched, blinded, poisoned, slowed, lured to a decoy, lured onto the follower itself) and shown in its own column on `balance.html`; their worth: stun, glitch and a decoy's lure the enemy's whole damage, a lure onto a follower **half** (moved, not stopped: the agent's, provisional), blind 50%, poison 40%, slow 25%; an enemy under several counts once, at the strongest. The damage a guard or a decoy takes is shown and **not added** again.
- **What one enemy deals** is reckoned (one bite on the trial's follower over the bite's cycle: 10.4 a second), not measured in a fight. The live runs are six of each (three before).
- **The trial run again on build `2026-11-14c`'s game** (the dash, the slow roll, the decoy's 2.75 s): ROTTEN DECOY 18.8 (all support: 36 enemy-seconds lured), SHELL GUARD 13.3 (it read 63.5), SWEET SCENT 15.9, MAELSTROM 40.2 (13.5 of it its blind). The game itself is not changed by this build.
- `tools/balance-trial.js`, `tools/balance.js`, `design/balance-trial.json`, `balance.html`, `docs/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`, `docs/DECISION-LOG.md`.

### Build `2026-11-14i` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · in a fight, the enemies' feet move as they close in
- **The report** (the owner, 2026-11-14): "The enemies don't move their feet when they're in battle and they're nearing an enemy or a follower or target."
- **The cause:** a creature is "attack" from the moment it locks onto what it is after, and it chases it in that state, step by step (js/predator.js); every drawing of its legs read "attack" as standing to bite, so its legs stood still for the whole approach.
- **The fix:** `creatureWalking` (js/bugs3d.js): locked on or idle, a creature walks while its walk cycle moves (each step of a chase moves it), and for 6 frames after; standing to bite, it stands. Hunting or wandering, as before. Every drawing of a creature's legs asks it: the 3-D bodies (ant, scorpion, spider, mantis, moth), the beetle, the older legs, the roly-poly's sheet and VOLTWYRM's.
- `js/bugs3d.js`, `js/beetle3d.js`, `js/draw.js`, `js/rolypoly.js`, `js/larva.js`; `tests/bugs3d.js` (an ant locked onto a follower walks every frame of its approach and stands to bite; every drawing asks the same).
- Seen in the real page (an ant's approach, four moments); not playtested.

### Build `2026-11-14e` (stamped 14b on its branch) · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the diving beetle larva: the electric breed's boss, in The Circuitry
- **The asks** (the owner, 2026-11-13): "a boss creature will be a predaceous diving beetle larva"; its look "A"; 2026-11-14: "The larva boss should be big", then "Build it" on the agent's proposal (docs/ELECTRIC-BREED.md §2, 9). Main took `14a` meanwhile (merged into the branch first).
- **THE DIVING BEETLE LARVA** (js/bosses.js, the row `circuitry`, on THE ANT QUEEN's pattern): asleep in the middle of **The Circuitry**, there whenever you come into the electrical domain (the domain's rooms arm it, so it waits before you reach its room); it wakes when one of yours comes within 2.5 tiles of its body or it is hit; held to its room (leave and it walks back to its post and sleeps, its wounds kept); killed, a fresh one waits the next time you come down.
- **Big**: twice the queen's size (about 4.5 tiles jaws to tail), a body 1.8 tiles round; twice her health (2133) at level 12; electric. Its own species (`larva`: a boss block only, the ant boss's numbers): no clone, no race, no damage row (every kind of hit ×1).
- **SICKLE SNAP** (her crush): its jaws open wide, edges lit, for 0.9 s, then snap in a wedge 2.5 tiles past its body (0.4 × its power, piercing, half of it electric), every 6 s at most. **SHOCK RING** (her stomp): its seams flash for 1.2 s, then a ring of current bursts round its body (0.25 × its power, all electric) and throws back whoever is in it, every 10 s at most, when one of yours is within 3 tiles. **No brood**: the domain's roly-polies keep coming.
- **Its look**: the mech of look A baked into `img/props/larva.png` (eight facings, a four-frame walk, its jaws wide and shut, its seams charged) from `design/props/models/larva.js`; js/larva.js draws it and picks the picture for each warning.
- **Also**: a tap anywhere on a huge body picks it (its body's reach added; THE ANT QUEEN's tap area grew to her body); the enemy card draws a creature too big for its old box in a bigger one (the queen's as before) and names its strength SNAP (SICKLE SNAP's; the queen's still CRUSH); the Clone Bay skips a species with no clone cost.
- **Provisional (the agent's)**: every number; the snap's Piercing; its name; that it pays nothing and unlocks nothing (as the queen). Kept from every boss: its shield aura on the roly-polies near it. Not checked: whether it can be shot from the Deep Server Room's ledge.

### Build `2026-11-14a` · branch `claude/extra-attack-looks` · the five extra attacks' looks, and how three of them play
- **The asks** (the owner, 2026-11-13: "he should just dash towards them and through them. It should hit multiple enemies. The Rift Impale hits one enemy but makes them glitch out. The way he does the animation is he glitches. The blade roll, he rolls up into a ball and rolls through at like a slower pace ... The Briar Sweep ... a bunch of leaves pop off of them, and leaves and thorns shake off and hit enemies in a AOE type situation ... Toxic Stomp, is a stomp ... make sure he splashes some of this Caldera"; of the first drafts, "Toxic Stomp and Blade Roll need better designs"; of the second, "Push").
- **EMBER SLASH** (read as the first sentence's subject; the message began mid-sentence): a **dash** 4 tiles through the enemies on its line, 1.2 × its attack each, burning each; it was one 2 × swipe at one enemy and a close move. A streak of fire behind it.
- **RIFT IMPALE:** the Mimic breaks into strips and vanishes for 10 frames, then a spike drives up under the enemy and it is there; the hit and the glitch land then.
- **BLADE ROLL:** it rolls its 4 tiles at 0.08 tiles a frame and slices each enemy as it reaches it (it crossed at once). Its look is a **3-D rendered ball** of its scales and spikes (`slugball`).
- **BRIAR SWEEP:** leaves, petals and thorns fly off it all round. Its play is unchanged.
- **TOXIC STOMP:** the Seeper's own leg-stomp frames; the hit lands with the foot, 12 frames after the cast; a **3-D rendered splash** of goo out of its caldera (`goosplash`).
- **On the preview:** the two 3-D pieces show with `?fx=3d` until the owner approves them in the game; everyone else sees the flat versions (the ones turned down). `_fxHideUntil` hides a follower's body for a move's moment.
- **Provisional (the agent's):** the dash's 1.2 ×, the roll's pace, the stomp's 12 frames. No body frames of their own yet (the Seeper's apart).

### Build `2026-11-13aa` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · the roly-poly: the electric breed, in the electrical domain
- **The asks** (the owner, 2026-11-13): a new breed, "a roly-poly type character that is immune to crushing, immune to slashing, and resistant to piercing. That will be electric type and will inhabit the electric domain"; as a mech ("take inspiration from Zoids and get rid of the wiry aesthetics"), longer, fewer plates, no eyes and no face, look **B**; "Clone potential"; "Physical half does 0"; "Curls up to defend"; then **"Build it"** on the agent's proposals.
- **The species** (`rolypoly`, ROLY-POLY on screen): always electric; a scout, a striker and a tank with a beetle's health, speed and size and an ant's bite; it bites with Crushing.
- **Immune** to Crushing and Slashing: their physical part does 0 (CRUSHING IMMUNE, SLASHING IMMUNE beside it); the elemental part lands. Resists Piercing; weak to Earthquake and Projectile. A new IMMUNE group (amber) on the enemy card. The player's lash and gauntlet do nothing to it.
- **CURL**: a hit that leaves it under half its health curls it into its wheel for 4 s (×0.3 of every hit; it neither moves nor bites), then 12 s before it can again; never stunned or asleep. Its clone curls too.
- **Its look**: the mech of look B, baked into `img/props/rolypoly.png` (eight facings, four walk frames, the wheel) from `design/props/models/rolypoly.js`; drawn at twice the resolution, a scout and a striker smaller.
- **Where it lives**: the electrical domain only (`SPAWN_TABLES.electrical`, `FP_SPAWN_ELEC`): three the first time you are there, then one every 25 s while you are, up to four. **Clone potential**: its DNA, and its clone at the Clone Bay (5 splices, 7 to 12 shards).
- **Provisional (the agent's)**: every number (its stats, the curl's, the spawner's), its weaknesses, ROLY-POLY as its name. No race emblem on its card (not drawn; open).

### Build `2026-11-13z` (stamped 13y on its branch) · branch `claude/energy-power` · energy cards give elemental power; any card unlocks
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-13: "since the energy cards don't really have much of an effect other than unlocking moves, I'd like to make it to where the energy cards increase the elemental power of the creatures ... a follower can have energy cards of another type and use those to unlock their other attacks, but those attacks would have very little elemental impact"; asked, chosen: **25 / 100 / 125 / 150%**, **"Everything, by card count"**, **"Hit damage only"**).
- **Power:** the elemental part of each hit an evolved follower lands with its everyday attack or a move is × **0.25 with no card of its own element, 1 with one (as it was), 1.25 with two, 1.5 with three** (`ENERGY.power`, `energyPower`, js/energy.js; applied in `dmgApply`, js/damage.js). The physical part is untouched. Not an Ultimate's hit, a status, a heal or a lure. An unevolved follower and a clone are not scaled.
- **Unlocks:** the cards in its slots **of any element** count: 1 the second attack, 2 the extra attack, 3 the Ultimate. A card of another element opens attacks and gives no power: cheap for a mostly physical form, a poor trade for an Elemental one.
- **What changes for a player:** an evolved follower with **empty slots deals a quarter of its elemental damage** until it holds a card of its element (saved followers too; nothing is migrated). A follower converted to BLOOM keeps its attacks open on its toxic cards, with no BLOOM power, and says so.
- **The words:** each slot says what its card unlocks and whether it gives power; the squad card has a POWER pill; the INFO card a POWER row; the INDEX the steps. "HYBRID · NOT DESIGNED YET" is gone (hybrids are for later).
- **The balance page** measures every form with three cards of its own element (power 150%), so its scores rose; the trial was run again.
- `js/energy.js`, `js/damage.js`, `js/unitinfo.js`, `js/infodex.js`, `js/cardsui.js`, `js/squadfly.js`, `js/conversion.js`, `tools/balance.js`, `tests/damage.js` (a check of the power; the rest of the suite at the baseline), `tests/energycards.js`, `tests/conversion.js`, `tests/squadfly.js`, `docs/ENERGY-CARDS.md`, `docs/COMBAT.md`, `docs/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`, `docs/DECISION-LOG.md`, the Bible.
- Not playtested.

### Build `2026-11-13y` (stamped 13x on its branch) · branch `claude/maw-jelly-fixes` · CHOMP's cooldown fixed; RIFT IMPALE glitches; the Thornwhip walks in for BRIAR SWEEP; the trial's knockback flaw
- **The asks** (the owner, 2026-11-13: "We need to sort out the Frostmaw's oddity behavior. And we need to increase Jellyram's attack if it's so weak"; "Thornwhip should walk towards the enemies when it has that new attack on"; "I like the idea of Rift Impale glitching the enemies as well"; the names "are fine for now").
- **The Frost Maw's CHOMP was a bug:** when the Maw had to walk in before biting, the move was read as "did not fire", its 2.5 s cooldown never started, and it bit again every 15 frames, up to three bites in the air at once (79 bites in 20 s where 7 were meant). Fixed in `_altFire` (js/evolution.js): a move that returns `true` fired. CHOMP's numbers are unchanged: 2.4 × attack a bite, the stun and the slow. In the trial it reads about 30 damage a second now (it read 337).

### Build `2026-11-13u` · branch `claude/balance-system` · the balance system: points, role bands, the coverage grid; the three energy steps
- **The asks** (the owner, 2026-11-13: "can we come up with a balance system to work with?"; then "Yes, I accept the points and the role bands as the system"; and of the empty cells: "fill the empty spaces with another attack on certain followers. Because there's 3 energy card slots ... if they have 3 energy cards, they can use their ultimate, and if they have 2 energy cards, they can use their alternate attack if there's an extra one. If not, the alternate attack is unlocked at 1 energy").
- **The three energy steps** (`ENERGY`, js/energy.js): 1 card of its own element opens its second attack (as before), **2 its extra attack where its form has one** (`alt2Needs`: a form's moves after its first, `evoMoveAllowed`; today the Static Orb's CAPACITOR DISCHARGE), **3 its Ultimate** (`ultNeeds`: it was 2). A form with no extra attack gets nothing more from its second card ("ONE MORE FOR ITS ULTIMATE"). The INFO card, the INDEX and the slot's words say it. **A follower holding two cards loses its Ultimate until it holds a third.**
- **The balance system on `balance.html`:** every mode's **points a second** (1 damage, 1 HP healed, blocked or added to an ally's hit = 1 point; an enemy held off = the damage it would have dealt: stunned, glitched or lured all of it, blinded 50%, poisoned 40%, slowed 25%); each form's **score** (its best mode) against its role's **target** (the middle score of its role today) and **band** (±25%), with a verdict; a **Flagged** list (forms above or below their band, and alternates scoring under 75% of their form's everyday attack); and the **coverage grid**, element by damage type, with its empty cells named.
- **The trial** (`tools/balance-trial.js`) tallies the enemy-seconds held off, weighted as the score weighs them, and what one enemy deals in a second when nothing holds it off (`foeDps`, from the live run).
- **Not built:** the five extra attacks for the empty cells (to be designed with the owner). Open questions: the targets (today's middles, not the owner's), the slow's 25%, what a hybrid card is for now that the Ultimate takes all three slots.
- `js/energy.js`, `js/moves.js`, `js/unitinfo.js`, `js/infodex.js`, `js/elements.js` (comment), `tools/balance.js`, `tools/balance-trial.js`, `tests/energycards.js`, `tests/balance.js`, `docs/ENERGY-CARDS.md`, `docs/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`, `docs/DECISION-LOG.md`, the Bible, `balance.html`, `design/balance-trial.json`.
- Not playtested.

### Build `2026-11-13t` · branch `claude/start-game-project-HSh7Z` · REEF is a climb only; a tap on the catwalks' east side makes the climb; the catwalks stay
- **The asks** (the owner: "Look, we can keep the catwalks where they're at, but the stone pedal just does not work with the reef on the right side. It works on the north side catwalk, but it does not work on the ... east side at all"; then **"We have to make it to where Reef can't be used anywhere else besides as a platform"**).
- **Why the east side failed:** the catwalks stand 5 tiles above the arena. A tap on their deck within about 2.5 tiles of the lip, where the ledge glows, was read as the arena floor hidden behind the deck, so REEF grew an outcrop there and spent its 60 s wait (or said TAP THE FLOOR). Only taps further in on the deck climbed. The Catwalk Spur, 1.8 tiles up, was read right.
- **REEF is a climb only:** its outcrop is gone. A tap on the technician's own floor (or on no floor) takes the ledge nearest the tap, within 6 tiles; with none in reach nothing grows, nothing waits and the aim stays up. The prompt is REEF · TAP A GLOWING LEDGE: A CLIMB; the ghost under the finger shows the climb a tap would make (or why not); the INFO card loses OUTCROP and MENDS; the tip shows the climb alone, its picture beside its words (the outcrop's picture is deleted, and its maker makes only the climb's).
- **REEF's aim reads the floor drawn in front** (`techReefScreenToWorld`): of the map's floor heights, the highest floor drawn under the finger, so a tap on a high deck by its lip is the deck's. The walking tap's pick is unchanged.
- **REEF PILLAR, the Ultimate, is unchanged**: its spikes keep the rock's rules (enemies held at the edge, shots stopped as at a crate, the mending).
- **The catwalks stay where they are:** the 2026-11-11 decision to swap them with the Observation Deck is superseded (never built).
- Tests: `tests/reef.js` (A CLIMB ONLY: no outcrop, the nearest ledge, a refusal costs nothing; THE EAST CATWALKS: screen taps at three depths on the deck along the whole edge each make the climb where tapped; the rock's checks moved onto a REEF PILLAR spike). Docs: `TECHNICIANS.md`, `CONVERSION.md`, `COMBAT.md`, `FACILITY.md`, `OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`, `DECISION-LOG.md`, the Bible.
- Not playtested; checked in the test sandbox (taps through the aim, as the iPad's are).

### Build `2026-11-13s` (stamped 13q on its branch) · branch `claude/balance-page` · the balance page; the Bible's follower sections without the history
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-13: "There's so much shit in the game Bible regarding ultimates and abilities because of all the changelog stuff. Can you simplify that for me? We're trying to make it to where we can balance everything because I can't keep track of all this stuff by myself"; asked, **"Both"**: a clean page, and the Bible's sections cleaned).

Read from the code, so it cannot drift; no history on it. `tests/balance.js` fails when it is stale, and `node tools/stamp.js` makes it.
- **`tools/balance-trial.js`** (new; minutes, not in the test run): the trial behind the measured table (`design/balance-trial.json`, with the build it ran on). Tallies damage, healing, blocked damage, assists, enemy-seconds lured and held. A second pass with enemies that fight back measures what SHELL GUARD, ROTTEN DECOY and SWEET SCENT block and lure. Its limits are on the page and in the tool's header.
- **The Bible:** the Ultimates section is rewritten short (charging, firing, reach, and a link; its stale "the Electro Skink has none" is gone); the forms and technicians sections lose their build-history parentheticals (43 of them), the retired moves' rows (PETAL BLAZE, ARC VOLLEY, SINGULARITY SHOT, PINBALL, the legacy "none" row) and the owner's quotes from the heavy table cells, and each points at the balance page. The Decisions blocks (collapsed) and the changelog keep the history. Nothing else in the Bible was touched.
- `tools/balance.js`, `tools/balance-trial.js`, `tools/stamp.js`, `tests/balance.js`, `tests/run.js`, `balance.html`, `design/balance-trial.json`, `bible.html`, `docs/DEVELOPMENT-FRAMEWORK.md`.

### Build `2026-11-13p` (stamped 13h on its branch) · branch `claude/personality-range` · a follower's stats stay within ±25% of the base
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-13: "make it to where the user's stats don't vary or stray too far from the original base stats"; asked how far, **"Within ±25%"**).
- **What it does:** every personality's factor on the base stats (`PERSONALITIES`, js/species.js) is held to **0.75 to 1.25** (it was 0.5 to 2.0). Each personality leans the way it did (aggressive and wild still hit hardest, cautious and stoic still tough, cunning still quick and accurate); only the reach is shorter. For example an aggressive follower's ATK is 12.5 (was 20) and its DEF 7.5 (was 5); a stoic's HP is 25 (was 30). The damage retune normalises by each class's mean, so a class's average damage is where it was; health and defence differ less between classes.
- **`assignRole`'s thresholds** follow, so each personality still gives exactly its role: a brawler's ATK ≥ 12 (was 14), a technician's DEF ≥ 12 (was 14).
- **Saves:** nothing is migrated. A follower already saved keeps the base it was made with; new recruits, eggs and offspring use the new factors. Breeding's cap (2× the personality's own number) can no longer be reached by the parents' mean.
- `js/species.js`, `tests/followertypes.js` (the thresholds), `tests/followerprog.js` (no offspring meets the cap now), `docs/FOLLOWER-PROGRESSION.md`, the Bible.
- `tests/battlelook.js`: the card-before-the-hit check expected the health as the rounded sum of the health after and the rounded number, which only held for whole hits; with the new stats it expects the health it began with (400).
- Provisional: the ±25% is the owner's choice of three offered; the exact factors are the agent's clamp of the old ones. Not playtested.

### Build `2026-11-13k` (stamped 13i on its branch) · branch `claude/blind-miss` · blind is a lower chance of hitting
- **The ask** (the owner, 2026-11-13: "Blinded doesn't mean that they don't hit. It means that they have a lower chance of hitting"; asked, chosen: **change blind everywhere**, **50%**).
- **What it does:** a blinded creature **keeps its target and still attacks**; each hit it deals **misses half the time** (`BLIND_MISS`, js/helpers.js `applyDamage`: a bite, a shot, an ability's hit, each on its own roll; a reflected hit cannot miss). A MISS floats over the target. Before, a blinded creature lost its target, could not attack or shoot, and staggered about at half pace (js/predator.js).
- **Who is blinded today:** MAELSTROM's swirl (a boss is still not blinded). Its INFO and the Bible say the new rule.
- `js/helpers.js`, `js/predator.js`, `js/unitinfo.js`, `js/evolution.js` (comment), `tests/evolution.js` (a check: 1000 hits each unblinded, blinded and reflected; the target kept), the Bible.
- Not yet built: the status effects on primary attacks and the damage-type coverage (proposed to the owner, awaiting approval). Lettered 13i; the branch `claude/personality-range` is stamped 13h.
